declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t609 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/async.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t611 = alloca i32
  %t610 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t609, ptr %t611, ptr null)
  %t612 = icmp ne i32 %t610, 0
  br i1 %t612, label %main.async.fail.58, label %main.async.ok.58
main.async.fail.58:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.58:
  %t613 = load i32, ptr %t611
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t613
}

  %t641 = load ptr, ptr %t640

  %t645 = icmp ugt i64 %t643, 0
  %t646 = and i1 %t644, %t645
  br i1 %t646, label %task.error.copy.59, label %task.error.empty.59
task.error.copy.59:

  call void @memcpy(ptr %t647, ptr %t641, i64 %t643)
  %t648 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t639, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0

  store ptr null, ptr %t649
  br label %task.error.msg.done.59
task.error.msg.done.59:
  ret ptr %t639
}
